# Verification record: user handbook (Feature 81)

Source spec: `specs/features/user-handbook.md`. Task list: `specs/features/user-handbook/tasks.md`.

This file is the verification record required by FR-11. Task 2 (this pass) is the baseline inventory only: every factual statement on the 21 old pages is listed as one row, with the release check filled in. The columns `Label`, `Runtime`, `Evidence` and `Outcome` are blank on purpose; Tasks 3 to 8 fill them by running each statement (`run` or `dropped`; outcome `kept`, `corrected` or `dropped`). No statement has been run, decided, kept or dropped yet. Nothing below states what the app does; the quotes are what the old pages say.

Method of this pass: `git`, `grep` and reading the 21 files under `docs/user/`, inside the command sandbox, no app started, no process started. Nothing under `~/Library/Application Support/getwrite-electron/`, `~/Documents/GetWrite`, `~/Library/Logs/getwrite-electron` or the repo's `projects/` was read or written.

## 4. Release check against `getwrite-v2.2.0` (FR-9, OQ-1)

Exit status of `git merge-base --is-ancestor <commit> getwrite-v2.2.0`: `0` = the commit is in the tag; `1` = it is not (merged after 2.2.0). "Introducing commit" is the first commit that added the file or string named (`git log --diff-filter=A` for files, `git log -S"<string>" --reverse` for strings; searched under `frontend/components`, `frontend/src`, `frontend/app`, `cli/src`). PR = the merge that brought the commit to `main` (`git log --merges --ancestry-path <commit>..HEAD`). A probe shows when the file or string first appeared; it does not show that every sentence about that feature on a page is new. Rows cite the probe that applies to them; Tasks 3 to 8 re-judge a statement that sits between two probes. "NYR" below = not yet in a release.

Context, read from the tag (not a run, not a verdict): `git show getwrite-v2.2.0:frontend/components/Layout/ProjectSettingsDialog.tsx` lists five tabs (Heading Styles, Body Text Styles, Default Revision Name, Manage Tags, Metadata); `git show getwrite-v2.2.0:frontend/components/preferences/UserPreferencesPage.tsx` renders `TimelineViewToggle` and `OrganizerCardBodySettings`; `ProjectFeatureToggles.tsx` at the tag holds the `entities` and Entity highlighting switches. The Project Settings tab names Editor, Timeline, Organizer, Writing Goals, Noise Words and Entities are therefore later than the tag (Editor `b29078aa8`, Writing Goals `d9625fe2d`, Noise Words `075fae063`: each `git merge-base --is-ancestor` exit 1). Pages written by Tasks 4 to 6 name some of these tabs; see section 14.3.

M-62 (providers) had no introducing commit in the inventory; `Folder scope` (RC-77) is in the tag, so the providers statements carry no note. Compile-this-entity (RC-50) and Entity Mentions (RC-68) were already exit 0.

Grep results on the old pages (measured): `repair-revisions` / `repair` does not occur anywhere under `docs/user/` (so no row and no "Not yet in a release" entry for it; RC-70 is recorded only because the spec's PR list names #262). `prune` occurs on `revisions.md` lines 25 and 39 and `project-configuration.md` lines 13, 14, 51 and 58; `reindex` occurs on `writing-log.md` line 42 only. Task 7's list of "prune, reindex and repair-revisions command statements" is therefore: prune and reindex statements as rowed below; repair-revisions has none.

## 5. `features.md` accounting (dissolved page)

`features.md` is dissolved (mapping table). Each paragraph is given a disposition. "dup:<page>" = the same claim is made on the named old page, so it is not carried; "intro" = assigned to `index.md` intro; "editor" = assigned to `write/editor.md`; "features-only" = no other old page makes it, destination named (kept only if later verified by a `run`); "contradicts" = the claim disagrees with another old page (the disagreement is recorded, nothing is decided here). The home-network sharing paragraph (lines 37-39) is excluded under FR-9: no rows.

Rows for `features.md` use IDs `F-nn` in the tables of section 6 (sections "index.md" and "write/editor.md" and "features.md, not carried").

## 6. Statement inventory (one section per handbook page)

Row columns: ID; statement (short verbatim quote); old page and line; release check (probe ID with exit status; `-` = no feature named that the probes cover); `Label`, `Runtime`, `Evidence`, `Outcome` left blank for Tasks 3 to 8; inventory note.

## 8. Summary for Tasks 3 to 8

- Rows per old page and the total: see section 3.
- Statements with no verification possible by the two FR-15 methods that the inventory already flags: installing steps (downloads, Gatekeeper, SmartScreen, Rosetta), signing and "planned" promises, the Android sentence, phone and touch gestures, native file pickers, the Scrivener and Word import buttons (desktop method only), the global noise words on desktop. They remain rows; Tasks 3 to 8 decide `dropped` with the reason.
- Contradictions between old pages found while inventorying (recorded as facts about the text, nothing decided): `features.md` ("quietly trims old history", "choose how much revision history to keep") against `revisions.md` and `project-configuration.md` ("does not prune revisions on its own", "no setting for a revision limit"); `features.md` ("two kinds of edges") against `views/entity-relationship-graph.md` ("up to five kinds").
- Pages that name commands: `revisions.md` (`getwrite prune`), `project-configuration.md` (`getwrite prune --max <n>`), `writing-log.md` (`reindex`). No old page mentions `repair-revisions`.

## 9. Task 3 record (getting-started and organize pages)

Task 3 filled the sections `getting-started/installing.md`, `organize/start-page.md`, `organize/project-types.md` and `organize/resource-tree.md` of section 6 above (74 rows) and wrote the four pages under `docs/user/handbook/`. Base commit used on all four pages: `b9ddb83c` (`git rev-parse --short HEAD` at the time; the working tree also held this task's uncommitted harness and doc files, no app code changed). Logs of the runs are copied to `specs/features/user-handbook/logs/task3/` (originals in `/tmp/getwrite-handbook/logs/`); the scripts are `scripts/task3-sec1.mjs` to `task3-sec6.mjs`, `task3-desk.mjs`, `task3-desk2.mjs`, `task3-lib.mjs`.

### 9.1 Rows by outcome

| Page | Rows | run, kept | run, corrected | dropped |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| `getting-started/installing.md` | 30 | 0 | 4 (I-21, I-23, I-24, I-25) | 26 |
| `organize/start-page.md` | 17 | 7 | 8 | 2 (S-16, S-17) |
| `organize/project-types.md` | 17 | 4 | 11 | 2 (P-02, P-17) |
| `organize/resource-tree.md` | 10 | 6 | 3 | 1 (R-08) |

The sharing sentence in the App Settings bullet of `start-page.md` has no row and is absent from the new page (FR-9). All 21 old files under `docs/user/` are untouched.

### 9.2 Methods and runtimes

- Web: `scripts/web-standalone.mjs` (built standalone server, throwaway projects folder, free port, 127.0.0.1) driven by Playwright Chromium, named in the rows as "web build of the same frontend". The server was started outside the sandbox, one at a time, and stopped by recorded pid after each run.
- Desktop: `scripts/desktop-dev.mjs` (Electron dev, fake repo root, `--user-data-dir`, throwaway app name), driven by Playwright `_electron`, named "desktop, Electron dev, fake root". The native file picker was replaced in the main process (`dialog.showOpenDialog` set by `app.evaluate`) for the import rows; the real OS picker window was not driven. Fixtures: the repo's `frontend/tests/fixtures/scrivener/sample.scriv` and a `.docx` built in the throwaway folder from the repo fixture's styles with three Heading 1 sections.
- Two harness changes made in this task: `web-standalone.mjs` gained `opts.copyTemplates` (a throwaway copy of the project-type files, so a Project Type Manager save could not reach `getwrite-config/`; no save happened, see D-1); `desktop-dev.mjs` now launches Electron with `cwd = <fake root>/electron`.

### 9.3 Defects and unexpected observations (recorded, not fixed; causes not established)

- D-1 Project Type Manager (web and desktop): the form is editable and marks "Unsaved changes", but no save or delete control was found; edits and a new draft were not written to the template files (throwaway copy) and were gone after reopening. The page (P-12 to P-16) and the Start Page's old claim of creating and deleting types are contradicted. Rows P-13 to P-16.
- D-2 Desktop (Electron dev, fake root): in App Settings, Add left the global noise-word list empty and showed "Could not save the noise-word list."; app log: "Failed to save the global noise-word list: ENOENT: no such file or directory, mkdir ''". Not run in a packaged build, so whether an installed app is affected is not known. Web build: the same list saved and persisted across reload. Row S-15.
- D-3 Start Page Manage > Package (web): the compile dialog listed 1 of 2 resources; the resource at the project root was missing. The in-project Compile Project dialog listed both (two runs). Row S-09.
- D-4 Start Page (web): after confirming Delete, the deleted project's card was still listed 2 s later and disappeared after a reload (two runs). Row S-08.
- D-5 Resource tree (web): a folder created inside Workspace through the New resource dialog did not appear until a reload (three runs). Row R-03.
- D-6 Resource tree (web): documents/images/audio created in one order were listed in another after a reload (two runs, Picture, Tone, Doc One against created Doc One, Picture, Tone); dragged order was kept. Row R-07.
- D-7 Desktop Word import dialog: lists type ids `game_writing` and `poetry_and_lyrics` as "Game Documentation" and "Poetry & Lyrics"; the create dialog and the Project Type Manager show "Game Writing" and "Poetry and Lyrics". Row P-05 and page warning.
- D-8 Desktop Word import (Electron dev): with the process launched from a different working directory the import refused with "That project type isn't recognized." for Blank and Novel; with cwd = `<fake root>/electron` the same file imported. The experiment separates dependence on the working directory; whether an installed app has a working directory that finds the project-type files was not measured. Row S-13 and harness note above.

### 9.4 Release check for these pages

Only the App Settings bullet of `start-page.md` (S-14, S-15; RC-25 and RC-26, PR #256, `git merge-base --is-ancestor` exit 1) carries `Not yet in a release (merged after 2.2.0).`; entry 1 of the table in section 7 is updated to match (S-16 dropped). The other 72 rows have release `-` or exit 0 and no note. No further entry was found necessary: the Package label, Manage menu, Import dialogs, Project Type Manager and tree operations were all present in the tag by their probes (RC-39, RC-55 to RC-57, RC-63, RC-71).

### 9.5 Ports, processes and cleanup

- Before every start: `lsof -nP -iTCP:7340 -iTCP:3000 -iTCP:6006 -sTCP:LISTEN` (or the script's own check): no listener on 7340, 3000 or 6006 each time. The owner's app was not open.
- Every web run used its own free port; every desktop run used 7340. All pids were recorded by the scripts (`Recorder`) and stopped by pid; each run's output ended with "listener after: none" and "removed: true" for the throwaway folder; the throwaway Electron log folder `~/Library/Logs/gw-handbook-throwaway-<run-id>` was removed by the script each time. `git status --short projects/` was empty before and after each run.
- `~/Library/Application Support/getwrite-electron/`, `~/Documents/GetWrite`, `~/Library/Logs/getwrite-electron` and the repo's `projects/` were not read, listed or written.

### 10.2 Methods and runtimes

- Web: `scripts/web-standalone.mjs` (built standalone server, throwaway projects folder, free port, 127.0.0.1) driven by Playwright Chromium on macOS (`navigator.platform` MacIntel), named "web build of the same frontend". Started outside the sandbox, one at a time, stopped by recorded pid.
- Desktop: `scripts/desktop-dev.mjs` driven by Playwright `_electron`, named "desktop, Electron dev, fake root", used only for the Word and Scrivener imports (W-36); the native picker was replaced in the main process as in Task 3 (`dialog.showOpenDialog`), the real OS picker was not driven.
- CLI: `node cli/dist/bin/getwrite-cli.cjs` (built in Task 1) against throwaway paths under `/tmp/getwrite-handbook/<run-id>/projects/`: `project import-plaintext`, `reindex`, `project create --spec <custom spec>`. Run outside the sandbox, from my scripts, never against any other path.
- W-16 method: the page's `Date` was replaced by an init script (Playwright) to 2026-10-15T02:00Z in three browser timezones; the server clock was real. W-42 method: the server process was started with `TZ=Pacific/Kiritimati` so its local date differed from the UTC date. W-15, W-38, W-39 and W-40 method: files inside the throwaway project folder (day file, `meta/writing-log`, a revision's `content.bin`) were replaced or removed while the server was running.
- No change to `web-standalone.mjs` or `desktop-dev.mjs` was needed in this task. The throwaway templates copy (`copyTemplates`) was used for `task4-sec2.mjs` but the HTTP project-creation route does not read it (the A-10 run used the CLI instead).

### 10.3 Defects and unexpected observations (recorded, not fixed; causes not established)

- D-9 Body Text Styles save and headings (web): after saving Heading Styles (H1 60px) and then Body Text Styles (font size 18px), project.json `editorConfig` read `headings: {}` and `body: {"fontSize":"18px"}`, the editor's H1 was 40px again and the dialog read 40 after a reload (`logs/task4/t4-sec2b.txt`). In `t4-sec2.txt` a Body Text Styles save also left `headings: {}` (the final run had saved H1 to H3 values just before). Rows H-06, A-09.
- D-10 Body line height (web): Body Text Styles "Line Height" values 2.2 and 1 were stored in project.json, and a custom spec with 2.4 was copied into a new project, but the paragraph's inline style stayed `line-height: 1.5` and the rendered height stayed 1.5; the stored default in a new Blank project is 1.8 while the rendered value is 1.5. The toolbar "Line Height" field did change the rendered value (1.2 gave 24px, 0.5 gave 10px at 20px). Rows A-05, A-06, A-09, A-10.
- D-11 Heading levels (web): a level added with "Add H5" and left empty was not listed after Save Changes and a reload; H4 with a size of 22 was. Row H-12.
- D-12 Project Settings (web): Cancel under Heading Styles closed the whole dialog; Save Changes left it open. Row H-14, H-06.
- D-13 Pipette button (web, headless Chromium): clicking "Choose H1 color" changed nothing visible; the native color picker cannot be observed here. This is an unobserved behaviour, not a defect found. Row H-11.
- D-14 Goal fields (web): after saving a daily goal (20) and a total goal (1000) and reopening Project Settings without a reload, both fields were empty. Rows W-19, W-23.
- D-15 Toolbar heading buttons (web): buttons Heading 1 to Heading 6 are present in a default project (settings list H1 to H3) and after adding levels. Row H-03.
- Earlier trial script (mine, not an app defect): a keyboard select-all left an empty selection in some formatting trials, so the toolbar click changed nothing; final trials check the selection string first and reload the page before each trial. Row F-09.

### 11.3 Defects and unexpected observations (recorded, not fixed; causes not established)

- D-16 Metadata queries, multiple choice (web): `includes all of Alpha, Beta` returned all three of Scene C (Beta), Scene A (Alpha, Beta) and Scene B (Alpha), the same as `includes any of`; the saved definition was `and[in fmulti [Alpha, Beta]]`. Rows Q-35, Q-50 (`t5-q3.txt`).
- D-17 Metadata queries, dates (web): `Created At in the last` 1 day, 7 days, 1 week, 1 month, `Updated At in the last 1 days` and `Created At is on <creation date>` each returned no resource for resources created or edited minutes earlier; `is before`/`is after` returned expected results. Rows Q-21, Q-22, Q-32 (`t5-q3.txt`, `t5-q5.txt`).
- D-18 Smart folder (web): after a resource stopped matching, the open list still showed it immediately and 5 s later; after clicking the folder again it did not. Rows Q-02, Q-38 (`t5-q3.txt`).
- D-19 Query builder, Tags (web): the `Tags includes` value box searches resources; typing an existing tag's name offered nothing and Enter left the value empty. Rows Q-24, Q-45, Q-01 (`t5-q2.txt`, `t5-q3.txt`).
- D-20 Query builder, Status (web): in a new Blank project (`config.statuses` is `[]`) the Status chip's value list held only `select...` while the sidebar offers Draft, In review, Published; with `config.statuses` set by hand it listed them. Rows Q-08, M-11 (`t5-q2.txt`, `t5-m1.txt`).
- D-21 Tags (web): tags created or deleted in Project Settings did not appear in or disappear from the open sidebar's Tags section until a reload. Row M-60 (`t5-m2.txt`, `t5-q2.txt`).
- D-22 Prose diagnostics (web): 6.5 s after typing and saving, the sidebar still showed the previous figures in 4 of 9 cases; after selecting another resource and back it showed the figures that were on disk. Row M-48 (`t5-m4.txt`).
- D-23 Custom Date field (web): its sidebar label read `Story Date / Time`, not the field's own label (`F Date`). Row M-21 (`t5-m1b.txt`).
- D-24 Built-in fields (web): renaming Synopsis to Summary in the Metadata Fields manager changed the manager but not the sidebar heading, also after a reload. Row M-20 (`t5-m1.txt`).
- D-25 Metadata Fields manager (web): the Status field's `Options (one per line)` box was empty and typing `Final` into it changed nothing in the sidebar. Row M-11 (`t5-m1.txt`).

Row M-04 (`t5-q6.txt`). Same family as Task 4's D-9 (a later save writing back a stale value) in kind; not investigated.

- Observation: `project.json` was not byte-identical before and after `Switch to dark mode`; what changed was not examined (row M-67).
- Observation: a resource created by `getwrite-cli templates create` sat at the project root (beside the `Workspace` folder in the tree), not inside `Workspace` (row M-46).

Contradiction between `features.md` ("quietly trims old history so it never piles up") and `revisions.md` ("does not prune revisions on its own"): observed, a document with 60 revisions kept all 60 (RV-20), and `prune` is the only command that removed revisions other than the per-card Delete Revision button (RV-19, RV-22); the page says so. All 21 old files under `docs/user/` are untouched.

Command rows: every `prune` statement on `revisions.md` (RV-22 to RV-29) was run with the built CLI (`node cli/dist/bin/getwrite-cli.cjs prune ...`) against copies of a throwaway project made inside the run folder under `/tmp/getwrite-handbook/`; each path argument was asserted under that prefix by `cli()` in `task7-lib.mjs` before the call. The executable name is `getwrite-cli` (Task 1). The `reindex` statement of `writing-log.md` was run in Task 4 (row W-45); the `prune` statements on `project-configuration.md` belong to Task 8. `repair-revisions` has no statement on any old page (Task 2), so it has no row and no page text.

### 13.2 Methods and runtimes

- Web: `scripts/web-standalone.mjs` (built standalone server, throwaway projects folder, free port, 127.0.0.1) driven by Playwright Chromium (1400 x 900, macOS), named "web build of the same frontend". Started outside the sandbox, one at a time, stopped by recorded pid.
- Desktop: `scripts/desktop-dev.mjs` driven by Playwright `_electron`, named "desktop, Electron dev, fake root", used once for Trash on desktop (TR-01, TR-04, TR-12). No native picker was needed.
- CLI: built CLI as above, run outside the sandbox from the scripts (the sandbox cannot write under `/tmp/getwrite-handbook/`).
- Revisions for the 60-revision scenario were made through the Revision Control panel (59 saves with names `M1` to `M59`, 26 s); protect clicks were made through the cards. Hand edits of throwaway files: `metadata.name` removed from a revision's `metadata.json` (RV-11, `t7-rv3.txt`) and `maxRevisions`/`autoPrune` in `project.json` of two copies (RV-28, C4 and C5). Prune scenarios ran on copies (`cpSync`) of the live throwaway project, never on it; the last step of `t7-rv2.txt` shows the live project unchanged.

Task 8 filled the sections `protect-and-finish/resources.md` (9 rows), `protect-and-finish/compiling.md` (12 rows) and `protect-and-finish/project-configuration.md` (34 rows) of section 6 above (55 rows), disposed the Task 8 parts of F-50, and wrote `docs/user/handbook/protect-and-finish/resources.md`, `compiling.md` and `project-configuration.md`. Base commit used on all three pages: `a07e14cc` (`git rev-parse --short HEAD` at the time; the working tree also held this task's uncommitted scripts, logs and doc files, no app code changed). Logs of the runs are in `specs/features/user-handbook/logs/task8/` (the final run of each script); the scripts are `scripts/task8-lib.mjs` (helpers), `task8-r1.mjs` (resources), `task8-c1.mjs`, `task8-c2.mjs`, `task8-c3.mjs` (compiling), `task8-desk.mjs` (desktop compile), `task8-p1.mjs` to `task8-p10.mjs` (project configuration). Three exploratory scripts used to find labels and selectors (settings tab dump, context menu and media view, move dialog and audio seek) were deleted and are not evidence. `t8-p3.txt` is the run whose examples step failed (hand-made project folders not listed, see PC-25); its exclusion step is the evidence cited for PC-23 and the examples were re-run in `t8-p4.txt`. `t8-p5.txt` is the first PC-29 run and `t8-p6.txt` the second (page text and console).
